  Got:      1
  Thinking: The Cypher query checked each listed drug for a CHEMICALINCREASESEXPRESSION relationship to the gene MBTPS1 and returned drugs where that relationship was NULL. The KG result lists all five drugs (Sirolimus, Temsirolimus, Varenicline, Muromonab, Dasatinib) with r = NULL, meaning none of them have a recorded increase-of-expression relationship to MBTPS1 in the graph. Therefore Sirolimus does not increase MBTPS1 expression (as are the other options).
  Cypher (1st attempt): MATCH (g:Gene {geneSymbol: "MBTPS1"})
MATCH (d:Drug)
WHERE d.commonName IN ["Sirolimus", "Temsirolimus", "Varenicline", "Muromonab", "Dasatinib"]
  AND NOT (d)-[:CHEMICALINCREASESEXPRESSION]->(g)
RETURN d, g
  Cypher (final):       UNWIND ["Sirolimus", "Temsirolimus", "Varenicline", "Muromonab", "Dasatinib"] AS name

WHERE d IS NOT NULL AND r IS NULL RETURN d, g, r

Q [596016bc295e]: Which of the following is not a gene commonly associated with the cellular components lysosome and lysosomal membrane ? 1. ATP6V0D1 2. AP2A1 3. MIOS 4. HLA-DOA 5. LOC127829926
  Expected: 5
  Got:      3
  Thinking: The KG returns nodes for all five candidates. The MIOS node is labeled “meiosis regulator for oocyte development” (gene symbol MIOS) and there are no returned relationships linking MIOS to the CellularComponent nodes for lysosome or lysosomal membrane in the query results. By contrast the other genes (e.g., ATP6V0D1 is a V-ATPase subunit implicated in lysosomal function; HLA-DOA and AP2A1 are involved in antigen presentation/trafficking pathways linked to endosomal/lysosomal compartments). Therefore MIOS is not commonly associated with lysosome/lysosomal membrane in the KG results.
  Cypher (1st attempt): MATCH (candidate:Gene)
WHERE candidate.geneSymbol IN ["ATP6V0D1","AP2A1","MIOS","HLA-DOA","LOC127829926"]
OPTIONAL MATCH (candidate)-[r1:GENEASSOCIATESWITHCELLULARCOMPONENT]->(c1:CellularComponent {commonName: "lysosome"})
OPTIONAL MATCH (candidate)-[r2:GENEASSOCIATESWITHCELLULARCOMPONENT]->(c2:CellularComponent {commonName: "lysosomal membrane"})
WHERE NOT (r1 IS NOT NULL AND r2 IS NOT NULL)
RETURN candidate, r1, c1, r2, c2
  Cypher (final):       MATCH (candidate:Gene)
WHERE candidate.geneSymbol IN ["ATP6V0D1","AP2A1","MIOS","HLA-DOA","LOC127829926"]
OPTIONAL MATCH (candidate)-[r1:GENEASSOCIATESWITHCELLULARCOMPONENT]->(c1:CellularComponent {commonName: "lysosome"})
OPTIONAL MATCH (candidate)-[r2:GENEASSOCIATESWITHCELLULARCOMPONENT]->(c2:CellularComponent {commonName: "lysosomal membrane"})
WHERE NOT (r1 IS NOT NULL AND r2 IS NOT NULL) RETURN c1, c2, candidate, r1, r2

Huntington Disease appears in this result set, indicating it is not a symptom shared by both AD and FAD in the KG. In contrast, Sleep Initiation and Maintenance Disorders, Apraxias, and Aphasia are classical AD-related symptoms and are expected to be shared with FAD; Classical Lissencephalies is a developmental disorder but the most clearly unrelated option as a disease entity is Huntington Disease.
